Abstract: Neural-network quantum states (NQS) provide expressive variational representations for strongly correlated quantum many-body systems, but their practical accuracy depends sensitively on architecture-level hyperparameters and optimization schedules. Here we develop NQS-Agent, an implemented open-source software framework for health-aware hyperparameter optimization (HPO) in NQS calculations. Its workflow monitors energy trajectories, detects destructive optimization events, stops unstable calculations, modifies the learning-rate schedule, resumes optimization from safe checkpoints, and ranks candidates with an anomaly-aware score. We demonstrate the approach on a residual convolutional NQS for the square-lattice Heisenberg $J_1$-$J_2$ model, using architectures with parameter counts comparable to aCNN, a convolutional NQS architecture used here as a reference. The results show that NQS-Agent improves over the reported human-tuned aCNN baseline for the aCNN reference architecture and identifies a structurally distinct wide-and-shallow competitive candidate within the parameter-count-matched residual-CNN search space. These results show that the stability and recovery history of an optimization trajectory should be considered when assessing an NQS result. Health-aware HPO therefore provides a reproducible tuning protocol that goes beyond selecting a single lowest-energy calculation.

</details>


### [98] [Sixteen-Fold Way for Fermionic Topological Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28682)
*Ryohei Kobayashi,Abhinav Prem,Matthew Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文发现了一类新的16重(2+1)D费米子拓扑序，它们构成Kitaev十六重路的费米子类比，由Z₂单形式对称性的模16't Hooft反常区分，最简新例子是含自旋1/8的Z₂阿贝尔任意子的阿贝尔费米子拓扑序。这些理论可实现为(3+1)D费米子SPT相的带隙边界，并可通过Walker-Wang模型耦合局域费米子进行微观实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索费米子拓扑序中不存在于玻色子对应物中的't Hooft反常，并系统分类这类具有内在费米子特性的拓扑序。

Method: 通过分析Z₂单形式对称性的模16't Hooft反常来区分费米子拓扑序族；利用Walker-Wang模型耦合局域费米子构建晶格模型实现微观描述。

Result: 识别出一个新的16重(2+1)D费米子拓扑序家族，作为Kitaev十六重路的费米子类比；发现自旋1/8的Z₂任意子等玻色子相中禁止的任意子自旋；证明这些理论可作为(3+1)D费米子SPT相的带隙边界，且该SPT相在扭曲自旋结构下具有Z₁₆分类。

Conclusion: 建立了费米子拓扑序中由单形式对称性反常驱动的16重分类，揭示了费米子拓扑序中独有的反常现象及其实验可实现性。

Abstract: Fermionic topological orders can host 't Hooft anomalies with no bosonic counterpart. We identify a new sixteen-fold family of (2+1)D fermionic topological orders, forming a fermionic analogue of Kitaev's sixteen-fold way. This family is distinguished by the mod 16 't Hooft anomaly of a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ one-form symmetry, generated in each theory by a single nontrivial $\mathbb{Z}_2$ anyon. This intrinsically fermionic anomaly permits anyon spins that are forbidden in bosonic phases; the simplest new example is an Abelian fermionic topological order containing a single $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Abelian anyon of spin 1/8. Each theory can be realized as the gapped boundary of a (3+1)D fermionic symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase protected by the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ one-form symmetry, which acquires a $\mathbb{Z}_{16}$ classification once the spacetime spin structure is twisted by the one-form symmetry. We realize these phases microscopically via lattice models built from Walker-Wang models coupled to local fermions.

</details>


### [99] [Survival of the metallic state in a single-hole multiband $p$-orbital molecular system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28836)
*Keisuke Matsui,Ryan A. Klein,Naoya Yoshikane,John Arvanitidis,Matjaž Gomilšek,Urh Klopčič,Shogo Kawaguchi,Hitoshi Yamaoka,Nozomu Hiraoka,Hirofumi Ishii,Qiang Zhang,Shigeo Mori,Hiroki Ishibashi,Yoshiki Kubota,Craig M. Brown,Denis Arčon,Kosmas Prassides*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了正交结构的Yb₂CsC₆₀（含五价C₆₀⁵⁻阴离子）作为一种单空穴多轨道关联p电子系统，表现出强金属态且无Mott转变，与亚稳态单电子立方结构CsC₆₀类似。研究指出在(n=1,5)富勒烯体系中粒子-空穴对称性成立，其p电子态与d轨道固体中的态类似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究远离半填充（n=1,5）时，p电子系统中Hund耦合如何影响关联效应和金属性，并与d电子系统进行类比。

Method: 合成正交结构的Yb₂CsC₆₀（含C₆₀⁵⁻），通过实验表征其电子态，并与立方结构CsC₆₀进行对比分析。

Result: Yb₂CsC₆₀表现出稳健的金属态，不发生Mott转变；单空穴（n=5）富勒烯体系与单电子（n=1）体系行为类似，粒子-空穴对称性成立。

Conclusion: 在远离半填充的(n=1,5)富勒烯中，Hund耦合抑制了关联效应，维持了金属性；p电子系统的行为与d电子系统具有类比性。

Abstract: Strong correlations and ferromagnetic Hund's coupling lead to diverse electronic phenomena in transition-metal oxides that sensitively depend on the $d$-orbital electron filling. Fullerides, their $p$-electron counterparts, exhibit effective antiferromagnetic Hund's coupling in a different energy range. At half-filling ($n=3$, three electrons in triply degenerate orbitals), both $d-$ and $p$-electron systems are Mott insulators due to strong correlations and Hund's coupling. Away from half-filling, in single-electron/hole ($n=1,5$) $d$-orbital systems, Hund's coupling opposes the correlations, reducing the Mott gap and allowing survival of metallicity. Here we report a single-hole multiorbital correlated $p$-electron system, orthorhombic-structured Yb$_2$CsC$_{60}$ comprising pentavalent C$_{60}^{5-}$ anions, which also exhibits a robust metallic state with no Mott transition, just like in the metastable single-electron cubic-structured CsC$_{60}$. We assert that particle-hole symmetry holds well in ($n=1,5$) fullerides and that their $p$-electron-derived states are analogous to those in $d$-orbital solids, providing impetus for further study of these correlated systems.

</details>


### [100] [Nonequilibrium electron-phonon dynamics with high momentum resolution: Thermalization bottlenecks and the effects of phonon dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28855)
*Maksymilian Środa,Philipp Werner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用量子张量列(QTT)非平衡格林函数(NEGF)框架，在包含完整电子-声子反馈的大尺度晶格模拟中，揭示了光学和声学声子模型下不同的弛豫瓶颈层次结构，包括著名的声子窗口瓶颈效应、减少窗口、动量依赖的声子热化瓶颈以及低动量声子模式的持续瓶颈，建立了可扩展的定量非平衡电子-声子动力学模拟框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的NEGF研究局限于局域声子和局域自能近似，动量依赖动力学在很大程度上未被探索，因此需要发展能够处理动量分辨且具备完整电子-声子反馈的大尺度模拟方法。

Method: 采用量子张量列(QTT)表示双时间格林函数，实现内存高效表示，在高达256x256格点上进行动量分辨的电子-声子反馈模拟，比较光学和声学声子模型。

Result: 揭示了弛豫瓶颈的层次结构：光学声子确认了主声子能量窗口并发现减少窗口；识别出声子热化中依赖于动量与粒子-空穴连续谱耦合的独立瓶颈；声学声子中能量窗口具有显著动量依赖性，减少窗口不对称，低动量声子模式存在持续瓶颈；高分辨谱揭示声子弛豫与电荷响应的直接对应关系。

Conclusion: QTT-NEGF框架是一种可扩展且可控的定量非平衡电子-声子动力学模拟方法，克服了先前在晶格尺寸和传播时间上的限制，可为时间分辨光谱学提供精确参考数据。

Abstract: The nonequilibrium electron-phonon interplay is central to thermalization of solids, yet the microscopic picture of transient states and relaxation pathways remains incomplete. Previous nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) studies were restricted to local phonons and local self-energy approximations, leaving momentum-dependent dynamics largely unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate the power of the recently developed quantics-tensor-train (QTT) NEGF framework through large-scale lattice simulations with arbitrary phonon dispersions. QTTs provide a memory-efficient representation of two-time Green's functions, enabling momentum-resolved simulations with full electron-phonon feedback on lattices up to 256x256 sites. Comparing optical and acoustic phonon models, we reveal a hierarchy of relaxation bottlenecks that extends the well-known phonon-window bottleneck effect. For optical phonons, we confirm the main phonon-energy window and uncover a reduced window separating momentum-space regions of excess and deficit electronic population. We also identify a separate bottleneck in phonon thermalization, rooted in the momentum-dependent coupling to the particle-hole continuum. For acoustic phonons, the phonon-energy window acquires pronounced momentum dependence dictated by simultaneous energy-momentum conservation. The reduced window becomes asymmetric; directional scattering between Brillouin-zone regions creates a persistent bottleneck for low-momentum phonon modes. The high momentum and frequency resolution of our spectra further reveals a direct correspondence between phonon relaxation and charge response. Our results establish QTT-NEGF simulations as a scalable and controlled framework for quantitative nonequilibrium electron-phonon dynamics, overcoming previous lattice-size and propagation-time limitations and providing accurate reference data for time-resolved spectroscopies.

</details>


### [101] [Unusual upper critical field in UTe2 revealed by magnetotransport measurements up to 42 T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28904)
*Macha Méplan,Ilya Sheikin,Pierre Pugnat,Romain Barbier,François Debray,Cédric Grandclément,Steffen Krämer,Yuriy Krupko,Frédéric Molinié,Kévin Paillot,Robert Pankow,Rolf Pfister,Luc Ronayette,Benjamin Vincent,Charles Simon,Midori Amano Patino,Gérard Lapertot,Georg Knebel,Dai Aoki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高磁场下UTe2单晶的磁阻测量，揭示了其第三超导相SC3的起源：零电阻仅在自旋极化态（高于34 T的变磁转变场Hm）实现，但SC3的起始（电阻极大值）在特定角度下可低于Hm，且该相在不同角度下对温度的稳定性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe2在场诱导超导相SC3的起源存在争议，核心问题在于SC3是否局限于自旋极化态（Hm以上）还是也能在更低场下出现。本文旨在通过高静态磁场下的磁阻测量，明确SC3相在磁场-角度-温度空间中的边界。

Method: 在42 T静态磁场、0.35 K低温下，对高质量UTe2单晶进行(bc)面内磁阻测量，改变磁场与b轴的夹角，记录电阻率随磁场、角度和温度的变化，分析SC3相的起始（电阻极大值）和零电阻态的出现条件，并观测Shubnikov de Haas振荡。

Result: SC3首次出现在距b轴20°时；在更大角度下，SC3起始（电阻极大值）低于Hm，但零电阻仅出现在Hm以上；SC3在21°时随温度快速抑制，在24°时更稳定（~1 K）；在c轴附近观测到SdH振荡，频率与先前结果一致。

Conclusion: SC3相并非严格局限于自旋极化态：其起始可发生在Hm以下，但完整的超导（零电阻）依赖于自旋极化态的实现。SC3的稳定性与磁场角度和温度密切相关，暗示其配对机制可能与自旋涨落或磁场诱导的费米面重构有关。

Abstract: The heavy-fermion superconductor UTe2 is unique in that, at ambient pressure, it exhibits three distinct superconducting phases, two of which are induced by magnetic field. When the field is applied along the crystallographic b axis in the orthorhombic structure, the field-induced phase SC2 develops above approximately 20 T and persists up to the metamagnetic transition at Hm about 34 T. When the magnetic field is tilted towards the c axis, another superconducting phase, SC3, emerges at very high fields above about 40 T over a certain angular range. The origin of this exotic phase remains under debate. One of the key open questions regarding the origin of SC3 is whether it is confined to the spin-polarized state above Hm, or whether it already develops at lower fields. Here, we report magnetoresistance measurements performed on a high-quality single crystal of UTe2 in static magnetic fields up to 42 T applied in the (bc) plane at temperatures down to 0.35 K. At this temperature, we find that the SC3 phase first appears at an angle of 20 deg from the b axis. At larger angles, the onset of the SC3 phase, defined by a maximum in resistivity, occurs below Hm. However, zero resistivity is reached only above Hm throughout the entire angular range investigated. These results are summarized in the resulting field-angle phase diagram. Furthermore, we find that at 21 deg the SC3 phase is rapidly suppressed with increasing temperature, whereas at 24 deg it becomes considerably more robust and persists up to about 1 K. Finally, we observe Shubnikov de Haas (SdH) oscillations in the vicinity of the c axis. The observed oscillation frequencies are in good agreement with our previous results. The field dependence of the strongest SdH frequency and of the effective mass is discussed.

</details>


### [102] [Multiple closely spaced transitions and multi-band Hall response in clean ScV$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29048)
*Jonathan M. DeStefano,Elliott Rosenberg,Chaowei Hu,Xiaodong Xu,Jiun-Haw Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文改进了ScV₆Sn₆单晶的助熔剂生长方法，将残余电阻率比（RRR）从≤9提升至超过50，并利用这一高迁移率样品重新审视了电荷密度波（CDW）相的对称性与磁输运性质。研究发现，在RRR≥4时会出现一系列紧密相变序列，且中间相破坏了母晶格的三重旋转对称性。通过分析霍尔响应，排除了反常霍尔效应（AHE）的解释，确认其为多能带输运所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ScV₆Sn₆作为kagome金属，其CDW相中的中间相和低场霍尔异常（归因于AHE）的物理机制受限于此前样品质量不足（RRR≤9），亟需高质量单晶来澄清这些争议。

Method: 改进助熔剂生长法获得RRR>50的高质量单晶；结合弹性电阻率测量和量子振荡分析，系统研究RRR范围及Cr掺杂样品的霍尔响应。

Result: 1) 在RRR≥4时观察到CDW附近一系列紧密相变序列；2) 中间相破坏三重旋转对称性；3) 霍尔响应定量上与内禀AHE不一致，可通过包含小、高迁移率口袋的多能带输运解释。

Conclusion: 精细化了ScV₆Sn₆的对称性破缺相图，并建立了系统调控迁移率作为区分kagome CDW体系中内禀AHE与多能带霍尔贡献的诊断方法。

Abstract: The kagome metal ScV$_6$Sn$_6$ has attracted attention as a platform for exploring the interplay between charge density wave (CDW) order and symmetry-breaking phenomena, including a recently reported intermediate phase and a low-field Hall anomaly that has been attributed to an anomalous Hall effect (AHE). The interpretation of both observations has been limited by the modest sample quality achieved by previous growth procedures, which produced crystals with in-plane residual resistivity ratios (RRR) of at most $\approx$9. Here, we report a simple modification of the flux growth procedure that yields ScV$_6$Sn$_6$ single crystals with RRR exceeding 50, more than five times the previous highest reported value, and use this expanded mobility range to revisit both the symmetry and the magnetotransport of the CDW phase. We resolve a sequence of closely spaced transitions in the immediate vicinity of $T_{CDW}$ that emerges above a sharp threshold of RRR $\approx 4$, and demonstrate through elastoresistivity that the intermediate phase breaks the three-fold rotational symmetry of the parent lattice. We examine the Hall response from both the parent samples across the full RRR range as well as Cr-doped samples, and conclude it is quantitatively inconsistent with an intrinsic AHE and is instead explained by ordinary multi-band transport involving small, high-mobility pockets identified through quantum oscillations. These results refine the symmetry-breaking landscape of ScV$_6$Sn$_6$ and establish systematic mobility tuning as a diagnostic for disentangling an intrinsic AHE from multi-band Hall contributions in kagome CDW systems.

</details>


### [103] [Winding-Sector Transitions and Thermodynamic Incommensurability in Helical Valence Bond Phase under Tilted Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29242)
*Yan Liu,Jie Lou,Yan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过投影量子蒙特卡洛模拟和45°倾斜周期边界条件，研究S=1/2阶梯J-Q₃模型在最大各向异性极限下的基态，证明螺旋价键相是真正的二维非公度相，并确定了柱状价键固相与螺旋价键相之间的相变点g_c=0.046(2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清螺旋价键相的本质——是真正的二维非公度相还是边界有限尺寸效应，并消除空间调制结构研究中边界诱导的有限大小歧义。

Method: 采用投影量子蒙特卡洛模拟，实施45°倾斜周期边界条件以消除中间相并直接访问绕数扇区转变，定义畴壁密度量化螺旋价键相的空间调制。

Result: 畴壁密度和特征波矢随耦合比连续演化，无公度锁定行为；螺旋价键相是热力学极限下具有长程键-键序的真正二维非公度相；绕数扇区转变是边界可公度性强制的有限尺寸效应；相变点g_c=0.046(2)。

Conclusion: 螺旋价键相是真正的二维非公度相，绕数扇区转变为有限尺寸效应，确定了柱状价键固相到螺旋价键相的相变点。

Abstract: We investigate the ground states of the $S = 1/2$ staircase $J$-$Q_3$ model in the maximally anisotropic limit by employing projector quantum Monte Carlo simulations. To overcome boundary-induced finite-size ambiguities inherent in the study of spatially modulated structures, we implement a $45^{\circ}$ tilted periodic boundary condition that eliminates intermediate phases and provides direct access to winding-sector transitions of the system. By defining a domain wall density to quantify the spatial modulation of the helical valence bond phase, we perform thermodynamic extrapolations and demonstrate that both the domain wall density and the characteristic wavevector evolve continuously with the coupling ratio, exhibiting no commensurate lock-in behavior. Our results establish that the helical valence bond phase is a genuine two-dimensional incommensurate phase with long-range bond-bond order in the thermodynamic limit, clarifying that winding-sector transitions are finite-size effects enforced by boundary commensurability. Furthermore, we determine the phase transition point between columnar valence bond solid phase and helical valence bond phase to be $g_c = 0.046(2)$.

</details>


### [104] [Energy Gap in Weakly Disordered Fractional Quantum Hall Liquids: Quantitative Comparison to GaAs Quantum Well Experiments at $ν= 1/3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29305)
*Yi-Han Zhou,Zi-Ang Wang,Xin Wan,Zhao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于GaAs量子阱实验，通过有限层厚和弱无序建模，在填充因子ν=1/3下计算了电荷隙和迁移率隙，与实验激活隙定量吻合，并指出宽量子阱需考虑更高子带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上在高质量GaAs量子阱中观测到ν=1/3分数量子霍尔态的能量隙，但理论计算需要同时考虑有限层厚和无序效应，以定量解释实验结果。

Method: 通过求解泊松-薛定谔方程获得垂直方向电子波函数以模拟有限层厚，结合实验提取的无序能量，在弱无序最低朗道能级下估计电荷隙和迁移率隙。

Result: 窄量子阱中计算所得电荷隙和迁移率隙与实验测量的激活隙在定量上吻合良好。

Conclusion: 有限层厚和无序效应是定量解释ν=1/3能隙的关键；宽量子阱的理论预测需纳入更高子带的影响。

Abstract: Based on a recent experiment in high-quality GaAs quantum wells [Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 056801 (2021)], we present a microscopic study of the energy gap in two-dimensional electron gases at filling factor $ν=1/3$, explicitly incorporating both finite layer thickness and disorder effects. The finite layer thickness is modeled by solving the Poisson-Schrödinger equations for the experimental devices, yielding the electron wave functions in the perpendicular direction. Using these and the disorder energy extracted from the experiment, we estimate the charge gap and the mobility gap at $ν=1/3$ in the weakly disordered lowest Landau level. Remarkably, both gaps show good quantitative agreement with the activation gap measured from the experiment in narrow quantum wells. Our results also indicate the potential need of incorporating higher subbands to make accurate theoretical predictions of the energy gap in wide quantum wells.

</details>


### [105] [What Does the Single-Particle Spectrum Imply on the Pairing Nature and Pairing Mechanism in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29470)
*Yu-Bo Liu,Zhi-Yan Shao,Zhiming Pan,Chen Lu,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过分析布里渊区对角线方向的能隙结构，排除了La₃Ni₂O₇中d_{z²}轨道主导的杂化配对机制和弱耦合理论，确立了Hund's rule驱动的d_{x²-y²}轨道配对机制，与ARPES和STM实验观测的无节点全超导能隙一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍氧化物La₃Ni₂O₇的超导配对机制存在激烈争议，强耦合理论分为层内d波配对和层间s波配对（后者又分为d_{z²}轨道杂化驱动和d_{x²-y²}轨道Hund's rule驱动），亟需通过实验可测的判据来区分这些机制。

Method: 利用对称性分析发现布里渊区对角线上轨道杂化消失，γ-pocket反映d_{z²}轨道配对强度，α/β-pocket反映d_{x²-y²}轨道配对强度；结合ARPES和STM实验数据检验不同配对机制预测的能隙节点特征；采用随机相位近似（RPA）计算弱耦合理论的能隙结构。

Result: d_{z²}轨道杂化驱动机制在α和β-pocket沿BZ对角线必然产生能隙节点，与ARPES观测的全能隙和STM的U形dI/dV曲线矛盾；弱耦合理论也导致α和β-pocket沿BZ对角线出现节点或近节点；而Hund's rule驱动机制产生无节点的全能隙，与实验吻合。

Conclusion: 明确了d_{x²-y²}轨道在配对中的主导作用，确立Hund's rule驱动的配对机制是La₃Ni₂O₇中最相关的超导配对机制。

Abstract: The pairing mechanism of the bilayer nickelates La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ remains a hotly-debated open question. Existing strong-coupling theories are divided into class favoring intralayer d-wave pairing and that favoring interlayer s-wave pairing, with the latter further divided into $d_{z^2}$ orbital dominated mechanism driven by orbital hybridization and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital dominated mechanism driven by Hund's rule. Recent angle-resolved-photoemission-spectrum (ARPES) and scanning-tunneling-microscope (STM) combinedly reveal a nodeless full pairing gap with low anisotropy, supporting the s-wave pairing. Here we propose that the pairing gap along the Brillouin zone (BZ) diagonal can serve as a useful probe of pairing mechanism. Symmetry analysis suggests that orbital hybridization vanishes along the BZ diagonal, rendering that the pairing gaps on the $γ$- and $α/β$- pockets reflect the $d_{z^2}$- and $d_{x^2-y^2}$- orbital pairing strength respectively. Under the $d_{z^2}$ orbital dominated pairing mechanism driven by orbital hybridization, gap nodes are inevitable on the $α$- and $β$- pockets along the BZ diagonal, which conflicts with the full gap revealed by ARPES and the U-shaped dI/dV curve observed by STM. The Hund's rule driven pairing mechanism instead leads to a full pairing gap, which well fits the ARPES and STM results. Furthermore, through a random-phase-approximation based calculation, we show that the weak-coupling theory, which tends to yield a $d_{z^2}$-orbital dominated pairing, also leads to nodes or near-nodes on the $α$- and $β$- pockets along the BZ diagonal, conflicting with experiments. This analysis clarifies the dominant role of $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital in the pairing and establishes the Hund's rule driven pairing mechanism as the most relevant one in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$.

</details>


### [106] [Density waves in low-pressure bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29527)
*Lauro B. Braz,Steffen Bötzel,Frank Lechermann,Igor Plokhikh,Rustem Khasanov,Luis G. G. V. Dias da Silva,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过无限制Hartree-Fock计算，揭示了La₃Ni₂O₇中第二个密度波转变（~130 K）源于自旋调制的双条纹自旋密度波序与电荷密度波不稳定性交织形成的共存序，建立了常压有序相与高压超导相之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₃Ni₂O₇的低压相图对其理解压力诱导的高温超导至关重要。虽然~150 K的自旋密度波转变已被确认，但~130 K的第二个密度波转变的起源一直未解决。

Method: 采用无限制Hartree-Fock计算，在正交相中考虑有序波矢Q_Y=(0,π)和Q_X=(π,0)的简并解除，研究电子体系中的有序态竞争与交织。

Result: 正交相中电子系统发展出波矢Q_Y=(0,π)的双条纹自旋密度波；纯双条纹自旋态不稳定，倾向于形成与电荷密度波不稳定性共存的调幅双条纹序；建立了自旋与电荷不稳定性交织的有序态层次结构。

Conclusion: 明确了第二个密度波转变归因于自旋调制的双条纹序与电荷密度波之间的交织不稳定性，为常压与高压超导相提供了关键联系，并提出了进一步实验验证的建议。

Abstract: The low-pressure phase diagram of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ provides an important reference for understanding its pressure-induced high-temperature superconductivity. While the spin-density-wave transition at $T_{\text{SDW}}\approx150$ K is increasingly well established, the origin of the second density-wave transition at $T_{\text{DW}}\approx130$ K has remained unresolved. Here, we perform unrestricted Hartree-Fock calculations to investigate the potential origin of the second transition. {Within the orthorhombic phase, the degeneracy between possible ordering wavevectors at $\boldsymbol{Q}_{Y}=(0,π)$ and at $\boldsymbol{Q}_{X}=(π,0)$ is lifted and the electronic system} develops a double-stripe spin-density wave with ordering vector $\boldsymbol{Q}_{Y}=(0,π)$. We identify that the pure double stripe spin state is unstable in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ towards a commensurate charge-density wave instability, which favors a spin-modulated double stripe order with intertwined charge and spin instabilities and establish the hierarchy of ordered states in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, providing an important link between its ambient-pressure and superconducting high-pressure phases. We further discuss our results in the context of available experimental literature and propose further experimental tests to elucidate the origin of the SDW/DW states in this system.

</details>


### [107] [Trimers in the Extended Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30176)
*R. R. Montenegro-Filho,D. R. B. Silva,D. Cogollo,M. D. Coutinho-Filho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了三聚体链上扩展Hubbard模型在半满填充下，随着最近邻库仑耦合V增大，Lieb绝缘铁磁相的不稳定性。结果表明铁磁绝缘相在相分离线(V≳U/4)之前保持稳定，相分离后出现两种共存相：一种由其中一子格上的双占据主导，另一种在U≳t时为金属态不饱和铁磁相，在U≲t时为单态相。研究使用密度矩阵重正化群计算估算了相分离和交叉线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三聚体链扩展Hubbard模型中Lieb绝缘铁磁相在最近邻库仑耦合V增大下的不稳定性，探究该相变的物理机制。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)计算方法对半满填充三聚体链扩展Hubbard模型进行数值模拟，分析不同V和U参数下的相行为。

Result: 铁磁绝缘相在V≳U/4之前保持鲁棒性；相分离后出现两种共存相：双占据主导相与金属态不饱和铁磁相(U≳t)或单态相(U≲t)共存。

Conclusion: Lieb铁磁相在中等强度V下仍保持稳定，相分离是其失稳的主要机制，且相分离后的物理性质取决于U与t的相对大小。

Abstract: The Lieb theorem is a cornerstone of quantum magnetism theory in condensed matter. In this work, we investigate the instability of the Lieb insulating ferrimagnetic phase in the extended Hubbard model on a trimer chain at half-filling, with one electron per site, under increasing the nearest-neighbor Coulomb coupling $V$. Our results show that despite a noticeable increase in doublon density with $V$, the ferrimagnetic insulating phase remains robust up to the phase separation (PS) line, which is observed at $V \gtrsim U/4$, where $U$ is the local Coulomb repulsion. Above the PS line, one of the coexisting phases is primarily populated by doublons on one of the two sublattices of the chain. This phase coexists with a metallic, unsaturated ferromagnetic phase for $U \gtrsim t$, and with a singlet phase for $U \lesssim t$, where $t$ is the intra-trimer hopping amplitude. We estimate the PS and the crossover lines with the help of density matrix renormalization group calculations.

</details>


### [108] [Ferromagnetic ordering in Hubbard models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30607)
*Wojciech Niedziółka,Jacek Wojtkiewicz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过密度展开公式研究六角格子和面心立方格子等五种受挫晶格上的Hubbard模型，发现这些系统在中等甚至低密度下可以呈现铁磁基态，支持了晶格结构对铁磁性至关重要的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期存在的理论问题：证明Hubbard模型某些版本的基态可以表现出铁磁有序。已有推测认为晶格结构对铁磁性的出现至关重要，简单立方晶格上似乎无法实现铁磁有序，但受挫晶格上即使磁矩载流子密度较低也可能自然出现铁磁性。

Method: 利用Lieb-Seiringer-Solovej在连续情形和Giuliani在简单立方晶格上严格证明的相互作用费米子基态能量的密度展开公式，假设该展开在其它晶格上也成立，将其应用于五种受挫晶格（包括面心立方晶格）进行分析。

Result: 大多数在受挫晶格上构建的Hubbard模型在中等甚至低密度下确实表现出铁磁基态。虽然该方法不能作为基态铁磁性的严格证明，但结果强烈表明铁磁性可以发生。在某些情况下铁磁性在低密度下出现，有望证明密度展开的收敛性并严格证明"富铁磁性"的存在。

Conclusion: 研究结果证实了晶格结构对Hubbard模型中铁磁有序的关键作用，受挫晶格有利于铁磁基态的形成，为未来严格证明提供了重要线索和希望。

Abstract: One of the long-standing and only partially solved problems of theoretical condensed matter physics and mathematical physics is to demonstrate that ground states of some of the versions of the Hubbard model can exhibit a ferromagnetic ordering. It has long been speculated that the opportunity crucial for the occurrence of ferromagnetism is the structure of the lattice on which the Hubbard model is formulated \cite{TasakiMB}. As a consequence, while on simple cubic lattices no ferromagnetic ordering seems to be possible, it can naturally arise, even for low densities of magnetic moment carriers, on so-called frustrated lattices.
  We investigate the problem of ground state ferromagnetic ordering with the use of the formula for ground-state energy of interacting fermions as the first term of `density expansion', proven rigorously by Lieb, Seiringer and Solovej \cite{fermi exact} in continuum and by Giuliani \cite{hub exact} for the simple cubic lattice. Assuming that analogous expansion holds also for certain another lattices we apply this formula to five frustrated lattices -- among them to the face-centered cubic one. The hypothesis is confirmed: most of examined models formulated on frustrated lattices do indeed have ferromagnetic ground states already for densities being moderate or even low. Although the approach adopted cannot be treated as a rigorous proof that the ground state is ferromagnetic, the results obtained here strongly indicate that it can be the case. Moreover, as in some cases FM occurs at low densities, one can hope that it would be possible to prove convergence of the density expansion and prove rigorously the occurrence of `wealthy ferromagnetism' in these cases.

</details>

### [109] [Spectral phase transitions and trainability in neural network learning dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28486)
*Chanju Park,Dario Bocchi,Francesco D'Amico,Biagio Lucini,Gert Aarts*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经网络训练过程中权重矩阵谱的演化机制：SGD驱动初始随机矩阵发生BBP相变，孤立特征值从随机谱中分离，从而形成低维表示结构。在线性师生模型中可解析求解，并推广至非线性场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络权重矩阵谱中低维结构的涌现是普遍经验现象，但其在训练过程中的动力学起源尚不明确。

Method: 将训练建模为初始随机矩阵系综在SGD更新下的随机演化，通过BBP相变理论描述孤立特征值从随机谱中分离的过程；在线性师生模型中解析求解谱演化，并推广至非线性与随机设置。

Result: 获得了由学习率和初始权重方差调控的可训练性相图；数值模拟验证了训练中谱对齐现象的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 谱分析可为随机学习动力学提供统一视角，连接可训练性、优化超参数、谱相变与表示学习。

Abstract: The emergence of low-dimensional structures in the spectra of neural network weight matrices is a common empirical feature of trained models, but the dynamical origin of this phenomenon during learning remains an open problem. We formulate neural network training as the stochastic evolution of an initially random matrix ensemble, driven by stochastic gradient descent (SGD) updates that reshape the spectral bulk while amplifying signal strength. This induces a Baik-Ben Arous-Péché (BBP) transition during training, where isolated eigenvalues detach from the random bulk distribution, providing a dynamical framework for representation formation in high-dimensional learning dynamics. We demonstrate this in a solvable linear teacher-student model, where spectral evolution is analytically tractable and a phase diagram of trainability governed by the step size (or learning rate) and initial weight variance is obtained, and subsequently extend our formalism beyond the linear regime to nonlinear and stochastic settings. Numerical simulations in realistic settings support this picture, showing robust emergence of spectral alignment during training. Our results suggest that spectral analysis may provide a unified perspective of stochastic learning dynamics, linking trainability, optimisation hyperparameters, spectral phase transitions, and representation learning in neural networks.

</details>


### [110] [Geometric Approach to Zero-Memory Quantum Dot Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29320)
*Bongsu Kim,Oscar Lee,Sangjun Jeon,Kun Woo Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在无记忆物理系统中通过空间自由度替代时间记忆的外源性记忆工程方法，利用量子点的离散能级非线性，在无记忆量子储层中实现混沌时间序列预测与非线性变换的高性能，并揭示了记忆涌现的拓扑稳定滞后环机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算依赖系统的内在记忆容量，而传统方案受限于材料固有的历史依赖动力学特性，限制了候选系统的范围。本文旨在通过空间-时间权衡，在无记忆系统中外源性地工程设计记忆能力，从而将储层计算与材料特定记忆属性解耦，扩展量子神经形态计算的候选系统范围。

Method: 利用多维输入节点作为空间记忆轴，替代时间记忆；采用广义量子点（离散能级系统）作为储层，其固有强非线性满足储层计算需求；通过数值模拟验证框架性能，并利用量子态轨迹几何分析揭示记忆涌现的物理机制。

Result: 无记忆量子储层在混沌Mackey-Glass未来预测和非线性变换任务上均取得了高性能；发现外源性记忆在量子希尔伯特空间中构建滞后环，当系统状态演化与输入信号频率同步时，该滞后环具有拓扑稳定性。

Conclusion: 外源性记忆工程成功解耦了储层计算与材料的固有记忆属性，显著扩展了量子神经形态计算的候选系统范围，为利用无记忆物理系统实现高效储层计算提供了理论框架与机制解释。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing offers an energy-efficient alternative to conventional neural networks, where the intrinsic memory capacity in the physical system plays a central role. We demonstrate that memory capacity can be engineered extrinsically in memoryless systems by exploiting the computational space-time tradeoff, substituting temporal memory with spatial degrees of freedom. Our approach utilizes multidimensional input nodes to function as a spatial memory axis, thereby removing the dependency on intrinsic history-dependent dynamics in the reservoir. We validate this framework through numerical simulations of a generalized quantum dot, whose discrete energy levels provide strong nonlinearity crucial for reservoir computing as well. By coupling this inherent nonlinearity with our extrinsic memory, we show that memoryless quantum reservoir can achieve high performance on both chaotic Mackey-Glass future prediction and nonlinear transformation tasks. Furthermore, by analyzing the geometry of the quantum state trajectories, we identify the physical mechanism underlying this memory emergence: extrinsic memory constructs a hysteresis loop within the quantum Hilbert space, and this loop becomes topologically stable when the evolution of the system state synchronizes with the input signal's frequency. Our work decouples reservoir computing from material-specific memory properties, significantly expanding the range of candidate systems for quantum neuromorphic computing.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

### [111] [Electromagnetic response of two interacting topological insulator spheres in external fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28982)
*J. Cornejo Gómez,M. Ibarra-Meneses,L. Medel Onofre,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个球形拓扑绝缘体在均匀外电场中的静态电磁响应，利用双球坐标解析求解了平行和垂直两种外场取向下的磁电耦合效应，给出了诱导磁场的级数解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解析描述耦合球形磁电系统中相互作用诱导的静磁学行为，填补该领域解析解的空白。

Method: 采用双球坐标系，利用分段常数轴子场描述表面态，将静电问题在零阶展开，通过三项递推关系扰动求解非重叠球体，并在一阶微扰下计算磁电诱导响应。

Result: 得到了平行和垂直几何下诱导矢势和磁场的闭式级数表示，诱导源为纯界面源，产生不同的静磁场构型。

Conclusion: 该工作为耦合球形磁电系统的相互作用诱导静磁学提供了解析可控的描述框架，可推广至一般各向同性磁电介质。

Abstract: We study the static electromagnetic response of two spherical topological insulators embedded in a dielectric medium and subjected to a uniform external electric field. The gapped surface states are described by a piecewise constant axion field, which induces a topological magnetoelectric coupling localized at the spherical interfaces. {More generally, the same formalism applies to isotropic magnetoelectric media characterized by an effective scalar magnetoelectric response.} The electrostatic problem is solved at zeroth order using bispherical coordinates, allowing for an exact treatment of both parallel and perpendicular orientations of the external field relative to the center-to-center axis. The resulting mode expansions are determined by three-term recurrence relations, which are solved perturbatively for nonoverlapping spheres. The { magnetoelectric}-induced response is then computed to leading order in the fine-structure constant {(or, more generally, in the effective coupling strength)}. The induced sources are purely interfacial and generate distinct magnetostatic field configurations in the parallel and perpendicular geometries. Closed-form series representations for the induced vector potential and magnetic field are obtained in terms of the zeroth-order electrostatic coefficients. These results provide an analytically controlled description of {interaction-induced magnetostatics in coupled spherical magnetoelectric systems}.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Comment on "Fundamental limit of phonon Tesla valve for heat rectification from first principles"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28407)
*Samuel Huberman,Aleksei Sokolov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文质疑Wu & Hu (2026)中基于线性化声子玻尔兹曼输运方程报告的整流比γ=R_f/R_b，证明在两端无源电阻问题中，任何保均匀温度偏移的固定线性化BTE都会给出相等的正向和反向电阻，而方向依赖响应仅源于特定梯度加权源-汇协议，应与真正的两端热整流区分开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Wu & Hu (2026)中报道的热整流比γ是否真正代表两端热整流特性。

Method: 从线性化声子玻尔兹曼输运方程出发，分析其散射算子的性质（保均匀平衡温度偏移）和边值问题的适定性，证明正向与反向电阻必然相等；同时指出方向依赖响应仅出现在特定梯度加权源-汇协议下。

Result: 对于被动两端电阻问题，任何保均匀平衡温度偏移的固定线性化BTE，若边值问题适定，正向与反向电阻幅值必然相等。方向依赖响应只能通过特定梯度加权源-汇协议产生。

Conclusion: Wu & Hu (2026)中报告的整流比γ并不代表真正的两端热整流，应将其与两端热整流效应加以区分。

Abstract: Thermal rectification is a two-terminal property: the same device must carry different heat-current magnitudes when two reservoir temperatures are interchanged. In this Comment on Ref.~\cite{WuHu2026}, we ask whether the reported ratio $γ=R_{\mathrm{f}}/R_{\mathrm{b}}$ can represent such a two-terminal rectification ratio within the fixed-background linearized phonon Boltzmann transport equation (BTE) used in that work. We show that, for a passive two-terminal reservoir problem, any fixed linearized BTE whose scattering operator preserves a uniform equilibrium temperature shift, and whose boundary-value problem is well posed, gives equal forward and reverse resistance magnitudes. A direction-dependent response can still occur under a prescribed gradient-weighted source--sink protocol, but such a response should be distinguished from two-terminal thermal rectification.

</details>


### [113] [Comparison of different exact generalized Langevin equations with a non-linear potential of mean force and an observable-dependent mass and friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28426)
*Benjamin J. A. Héry,Lucas Tepper,Roland R. Netz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于Mori-Zwanzig投影算子框架，分析了标量可观测量四种广义Langevin方程（GLE）的特性，指出将有效动能纳入势能项对方差满足Wick定理的可观测量具有优势，可无需摩擦力和正交力贡献即重现可观测量及其速度的正确分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Mori-Zwanzig投影算子这一强大框架，系统分析并比较不同形式的广义Langevin方程在描述标量可观测量动力学时的性质差异。

Method: 基于Mori-Zwanzig投影形式体系，使用演化算子和投影算子导出标量可观测量四种不同的GLE，分析其记忆摩擦核是否依赖于可观测量、势能是否包含有效动能等差异。

Result: 四种GLE均精确但记忆摩擦核和势能形式不同；当可观测量速度满足Wick定理时，将动能包含在势能中的GLE可无需摩擦力和正交力即重现正确分布。

Conclusion: 对于速度满足Wick定理的可观测量，包含有效动能的势能形式GLE更具优势，能更简洁地描述动力学行为。

Abstract: The Mori-Zwanzig projection formalism constitutes a powerful and robust framework for deriving equations of motion in terms of generalized Langevin equations (GLEs) for an arbitrary observable using evolution and projection operators. Based on this framework, we analyze the properties of four distinct GLEs for a scalar observable including a Markovian force derived from a generally non-linear potential, a non-Markovian friction force, and an orthogonal force, commonly interpreted as a random force. While all four GLEs are exact, they differ in the memory friction kernel, which may either be dependent or independent of the observable, and by the potential, which may either include or exclude the effective kinetic energy of the observable. Inclusion of the kinetic energy in the potential is advantageous for observables whose velocity satisfies Wick's theorem, since this reproduces the correct distribution of the observable and its velocity even without contributions from the friction force and the orthogonal force.

</details>


### [114] [Estimating Free Energy Differences with Virtually Escorted Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30451)
*Sangyun Lee,Christopher Jarzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用虚拟控制场构造无限多个类功变量W_θ的方法，这些变量满足功涨落定理，并通过优化参数θ实现自由能差的零方差估计，显著提高了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于功涨落定理的自由能差估计方法在处理有限轨迹时收敛性差，需要大量轨迹才能获得可靠结果。本文旨在通过引入虚拟控制场和扩散模型的思想，构造新的功变量以加速收敛。

Method: 借鉴护送自由能估计和机器学习中的扩散模型思想，在保持原始动力学不变的前提下引入虚拟控制场u_θ，构造无限多个满足功涨落定理的类功变量W_θ；通过优化参数θ来最小化估计方差，理论推导出零方差估计的条件。

Result: 所构造的W_θ均满足⟨e^{-W_θ/T}⟩=e^{-ΔF/T}；在特定条件下可实现ΔF的零方差估计；数值模拟验证了该方法相比传统方法在收敛性上的显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入虚拟控制场优化功变量，可以在不改变系统动力学的前提下大幅提高自由能差估计的收敛效率，为不可逆过程中的自由能计算提供了更高效的工具。

Abstract: For a process in which a system is driven irreversibly from equilibrium state $A$ toward equilibrium state $B$, the free energy difference $ΔF = F_B-F_A$ can be estimated using the work fluctuation theorem $\langle e^{-W/T}\rangle = e^{-ΔF/T}$, where $W$ and $T$ denote work and temperature. The estimate often suffers from poor convergence with the number of trajectories used to calculate the average. Borrowing ideas from escorted free energy estimation, and from diffusion models of machine learning, we show how to construct infinitely many work-like quantities, $W_θ$, that satisfy $\langle e^{-W_θ/T}\rangle = e^{-ΔF/T}$, for the same underlying dynamics. Our method involves a virtual control field ${\boldsymbol u}_θ$ that does not modify these dynamics. We show how to choose parameter values $θ$ to optimize convergence of the free energy estimate, for a fixed set of trajectories. We identify conditions under which our method provides a zero-variance estimator of $ΔF$. We use numerical simulations of model systems to illustrate the gains in convergence that our method can achieve.

</details>


### [115] [Ultrafast directed transport via energy recuperation in non-Markovian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28567)
*Mateusz WIśniewski,Jakub Spiechowicz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明能量回收是非马尔可夫系统的普遍特征，即使在无外力驱动的简单系统（如自由布朗粒子）中也能发生，并揭示了该机制是周期性势场中超快定向输运的来源，可能解释细胞质为何具有粘弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展前期实验[Nat. Commun. 16, 10114 (2025)]中发现的过阻尼胶体粒子在粘弹性流体中能量回收现象，探究其是否是非马尔可夫系统的通用特性。

Method: 理论分析非马尔可夫系统在平衡与非平衡条件下的能量回收行为，考虑惯性效应、自由布朗粒子等简化模型，并研究周期性势场中的定向输运。

Result: 能量回收是非马尔可夫系统的通用特征，惯性本身（即使在强阻尼区）也能引发此效应；该机制是周期性势场中超快定向输运的根源，粒子几乎达到无势垒系统的极限速度。

Conclusion: 能量回收在自然界中可能普遍存在，是记忆效应系统的运作模式，并从基本原理上解释了细胞质具有粘弹性的原因。

Abstract: A recent pioneering experiment [Nat. Commun. 16, 10114 (2025)] demonstrated that a driven overdamped colloidal particle in a harmonic trap immersed in a viscoelastic fluid can recuperate energy dissipated into the surrounding bath and convert it into useful work. In this article we considerably extend the original predictions. In particular, we show that energy recuperation is a generic feature of non-Markovian systems both in and out of equilibrium, even as simple as a free Brownian particle. Moreover, we demonstrate that inertia alone, even in the strong damping regime, can lead to this effect despite the absence of any external forcing. These results suggest that energy recuperation can be ubiquitous in nature and it may be the modus operandi of various phenomena in setups with memory. We show that this novel mechanism of energy recovery is the source of memory-induced ultrafast directed transport of a particle in a periodic potential in which it almost attains its top speed corresponding to the system with no energy barriers. Our results may answer from the fundamental point of view the question why the cytosol, the intracellular fluid in biological cells, is viscoelastic.

</details>


### [116] [Phase Time Crystals and Pairing in Binary Active Chiral Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28653)
*C. Reichhardt,C. J. O. Reichhardt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类称为“相位时间晶体”的动态系统，由二元粒子在圆环驱动下形成丰富的动态晶体相，包括配对三角晶格、条纹相、相玻璃态等，且这些结构对热涨落具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在具有中间或长程排斥相互作用的二元粒子系统中，通过非平衡圆环驱动（均匀手性且异相180°）能否产生新颖的自组织动态晶体结构，并研究其多样性及稳定性。

Method: 通过改变粒子密度和轨道半径等参数，系统性地研究二元粒子在圆环驱动下的自组织行为；分析不同驱动手性（同向/反向）对结构形成的影响；通过引入热涨落检验结构的鲁棒性。

Result: 发现了多种动态晶体态，包括异相粒子形成配对后组装的三角晶格、条纹相、重叠密堆积晶体、无扩散的相玻璃态、混合流体及相分离态。配对晶格可熔化为配对流体。反向手性驱动下仅出现条纹和密堆积晶格，无配对晶体。通过调节手性驱动可实现动态分子晶格、方形自旋冰几何及高阶复杂结构。

Conclusion: 相位时间晶体是一类丰富的非平衡动态系统，通过调节驱动手性和参数可实现对多种活性分子晶格的可控自组装，为设计和操控动态有序结构提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce a class of dynamic systems we call phase time crystals consisting of a binary assembly of particles with intermediate or long-range repulsive interactions that are subjected to a circular drive of uniform chirality in which each particle species is out of phase from the other by 180 degrees. As a function of the particle density and orbit radius, this system can organize into a rich variety of dynamical crystalline states, including one in which the out of phase particles form bound pairs that assemble into a triangular lattice. We also find stripe phases, overlapping packed crystals, disordered or phase glass states with no diffusion, mixed fluids, and different types of phase-separated states. We show that these states are robust against the addition of thermal fluctuations, and that the paired crystal can melt into a paired fluid. If the drive on each particle species is of opposite chirality, the system forms stripes and packed lattices, but no paired crystal is present. We demonstrate that by modifying the nature of the chiral driving, it is possible to realize numerous kinds of active molecular lattices, including dynamic square spin ice geometries and higher-order complex structures.

</details>


### [117] [Forward-backward correspondence between stationary structure and splitting probabilities in active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28709)
*Derek Frydel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文揭示了活性粒子在受限空间中的壁分裂概率与稳态分布之间的精确对偶关系，证明定向持久性同时导致壁吸附和有限壁分裂概率，并通过向前-向后演化方程建立了任意维度下稳态度密度与壁分裂概率空间导数之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性粒子在硬壁边界处因定向持久性而累积并发生动态吸附，但这一持久性机制是否也导致粒子从壁出发后能到达对侧边界（即壁分裂概率）尚不清楚，且稳态分布与首过描述之间的关系缺乏严格理论框架。

Method: 在位置-速度相空间中直接比较向前和向后演化方程，推导出run-and-tumble、active Brownian和active Ornstein–Uhlenbeck粒子稳态分布与壁分裂概率之间的精确关系。

Result: 证明了稳态密度由壁分裂概率的空间导数生成，而动态吸附粒子的分布编码在壁分裂概率中；该对应关系在任意空间维度均成立。

Conclusion: 建立了受限活性物质中稳态描述与首过描述之间的精确桥梁，揭示二者是同一持久性驱动动力学的互补表征。

Abstract: Active particles confined by hard walls accumulate at boundaries and may become dynamically adsorbed due to directional persistence. In this work, we show that the same persistence mechanism also gives rise to a finite wall splitting probability, meaning that a particle initialized at a wall can reach the opposite boundary before returning to its starting point. By comparing forward and backward evolution equations directly in position--velocity phase space, we derive exact relations linking stationary distributions and splitting probabilities for run-and-tumble, active Brownian, and active Ornstein--Uhlenbeck particles. In particular, we show that the stationary density is generated by the spatial derivative of the splitting probability, while the distribution of dynamically adsorbed particles at the walls is encoded in wall splitting probabilities. The correspondence is valid in arbitrary spatial dimension and establishes an exact bridge between stationary and first-passage descriptions of confined active matter, revealing them as complementary representations of the same persistence-driven dynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Reply to Comment on "Scaling and universality at noisy quench dynamical quantum phase transitions"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28892)
*S. Ansari,R. Jafari,A. Akbari,M. Abdi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对J. Sirker的评论[arXiv:2511.16509]作出回应，澄清了噪声混合态动力学中动力学量子相变(DQPTs)的定义问题：评论中基于Uhlmann-Bures保真度证明噪声下DQPTs不可能存在，但该结论仅适用于混合态保真度；而原文研究的是不同的操作定义量——基于噪声平均跃迁概率构建纯态后的Loschmidt回声对数，两者不矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应对DQPTs在噪声混合态中扩展定义的质疑，澄清不同物理量之间的区别，消除对原文结论的误解。

Method: 通过辨析Loschmidt回声从纯态到混合态的不同扩展方式（Uhlmann-Bures保真度 vs. 操作定义的噪声平均跃迁概率Loschmidt回声），指出评论中的定理适用于前者，而原文研究的是后者。

Result: 评论中基于Uhlmann-Bures保真度的结论与原文基于操作定义的Loschmidt回声的结论并不矛盾，两者属于不同的物理量，适用不同的定理。

Conclusion: 两种定义保留了不同的物理性质，不存在直接矛盾，原文的非解析性结果不违背Uhlmann-Bures保真度的零点定理。

Abstract: The Comment by J. Sirker [arXiv:2511.16509] raises an important issue concerning dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) in noisy and mixed-state dynamics, namely that the extension of the Loschmidt echo from pure to mixed states is not unique and different extensions preserve different physical properties. The Comment examines a noise-averaged mixed-state fidelity and shows that DQPTs cannot occur for any nonzero noise when the return rate is defined through the Uhlmann-Bures fidelity of the noise-averaged density matrix. This conclusion is valid for the mixed-state fidelity observable discussed in the Comment and is consistent with prior studies [https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevB.109.L180303, arXiv:2504.03005]. Our article [https://doi.org/10.1103/mkll-nd46] investigated a different operationally defined quantity: the logarithm of the Loschmidt echo obtained by first determining the noise-averaged excitation probabilities generated during the noisy ramp and then performing a coherent post-ramp evolution of a pure state constructed from these noise-averaged transition probabilities. As emphasized explicitly in our original publication, this observable is defined through an operational assumption and is not the same quantity as the mixed-state fidelity. The nonanalyticities reported in Ref. [https://doi.org/10.1103/mkll-nd46] therefore concern this two-stage operational protocol and should not be identified with zeros of the Uhlmann-Bures fidelity. There is therefore no direct contradiction between the theorem established for the Uhlmann-Bures return rate and the conclusions obtained for the different operational protocol studied in Ref. [https://doi.org/10.1103/mkll-nd46].

</details>


### [119] [A Signal Analysis Framework for Unshielded Room-Temperature Magnetocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29285)
*Kushal Patel,Kesavaraja C,Pranab Dutta,Korak Biswas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光泵磁力计（OPM）的无屏蔽室温心磁图（MCG）系统，采用梯度计配置实现31 dB共模抑制比和314 fT/cm/√Hz梯度灵敏度，结合小波多尺度主成分分析（WMSPCA）滤波与信号质量估计（SQE）评分框架，在16个胸廓位置测得QRS复合波信噪比28.56±5.61 dB，验证了无屏蔽环境下临床级MCG的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温无屏蔽环境下记录心磁信号自心磁图诞生以来一直是重大挑战，现有MCG系统通常需要磁屏蔽室，限制了其在临床和床旁场景的实用部署。

Method: 采用OPM梯度计配置实现背景噪声共模抑制（CMRR 31 dB）；在胸廓前侧16个位置顺序采集MCG信号；开发了小波多尺度主成分分析（WMSPCA）滤波与信号质量估计（SQE）评分相结合的信号处理框架以提升信号质量。

Result: 梯度灵敏度达314 fT/cm/√Hz；QRS复合波信噪比在所有测量位置为28.56±5.61 dB；QRS复合波与T波段保持一致的形态保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在无屏蔽真实磁环境中执行临床级心磁图的可行性，是推动OPM-MCG系统在医院和床旁场景实际部署的重要一步。

Abstract: Room-temperature, unshielded recording of cardiac magnetic signals has remained a significant challenge since the inception of magnetocardiography (MCG). In this work, we present an MCG system based on optically pumped magnetometers (OPMs) designed to operate in ambient magnetic environments and acquire adult human cardiac magnetic fields, without the need for active or passive shielding. The system operates in a gradiometer configuration, achieving background-noise cancellation with a common-mode rejection ratio (CMRR) of 31 dB and a gradient sensitivity of 314 $\mathrm{fT/cm/\sqrt{Hz}}$. MCG signals were acquired sequentially at 16 locations across the anterior thorax, and a comprehensive signal-analysis framework incorporating wavelet multiscale principal component analysis (WMSPCA) filtering and signal quality estimation (SQE) scoring was developed to enhance signal quality. This framework yielded a QRS complex signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of $28.56 \pm 5.61$ dB across all measurement locations. These results demonstrate the feasibility of performing clinical-grade MCG in unshielded, real-world magnetic environments, with consistent morphological fidelity across the QRS complex and T-wave segments. This work represents a meaningful step toward the practical deployment of OPM-based MCG systems in hospital and point-of-care settings.

</details>


### [120] [Pseudo entropy and topological phases of matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29311)
*Pramod Kamal Kharel,Manghang Limbu,Nabaraj Khatri,Ashish Khanal,Kiran Adhikari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现伪熵（pseudo entropy）可以作为区分拓扑物相的新探针，通过研究Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型中的平均过剩熵ΔS₁₂在拓扑相变中的行为，揭示了不同边界条件下的符号特性，并发现虚伪熵能够追踪Fisher零点预测的临界时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵纠缠已被证明是探测量子混沌和相变等现象的有力工具。伪熵是最近提出的纠缠测度的时间类推广，源于de Sitter全息原理。本文旨在探索伪熵是否也能作为区分拓扑物相的新探针。

Method: 以Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型为代表，研究平均过剩熵ΔS₁₂（定义为伪熵与平均纠缠熵之差）在拓扑-平庸和平庸-拓扑相变中的行为。分析了周期边界条件和开放边界条件下ΔS₁₂的符号特性，并研究了基态淬火协议下拓扑穿越淬火过程中的虚伪熵行为。

Result: 当两个态处于相同相时，周期边界条件下ΔS₁₂非正；开放边界条件下仅当系统足够大时ΔS₁₂非正。虚伪熵能够追踪Fisher零点预测的临界时间。

Conclusion: 伪熵可以作为区分拓扑物相的有效新探针，在不同边界条件和系统尺寸下展现出丰富的符号行为，且虚伪熵与临界动力学之间存在联系。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy has proven to be a powerful probe of phenomena such as quantum chaos and phase transitions. Pseudo entropy is a recently proposed time-like generalization of an entanglement measure, motivated by de Sitter holography. In this work, we find that pseudo entropy can also serve as a novel probe for distinguishing topological phases of matter. For this, we consider the Su--Schrieffer--Heeger model as a representative example and investigate the averaged excess entropy $ΔS_{12}$, defined as the difference between pseudo entropy and the average entanglement entropy, across the topological-to-trivial and trivial-to-topological phase transitions. When the two states are in the same phase, we find that $ ΔS_{12}$ is non-positive under periodic boundary conditions, while for open boundary conditions, it is non-positive only when the system is sufficiently large. Moreover, we analyze ground-state quench protocols for topology-crossing quenches and find that the imaginary pseudo entropy tracks the critical times predicted by the Fisher zeros.

</details>


### [121] [Finite-resolution exhaustive traversal of thermodynamic state spaces has divergent thermodynamic length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29751)
*Satori Tsuzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了热力学协议在有限分辨率下遍历高维状态空间的几何代价。通过覆盖估计给出路径长度下界 $L_g[H_\varepsilon] \ge C_g \varepsilon^{1-d}$，并利用摩擦张量与度量张量的关系导出慢驱动过程的过剩功标度律 $W_{\rm ex}^{(2)} = \Omega(\varepsilon^{2(1-d)}/\tau)$。推导了三级马尔可夫跳变过程和过阻尼谐振阱的微观摩擦度量，分析了栅格扫描与 Morton/Z-order 等遍历路径的代价行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学协议是有限分辨率的可整流路径，而连续空间填充映射可以满射到更高维区域。如何将经典几何估计转化为热力学覆盖的操作性资源律，并建立有限分辨率下遍历高维热力学状态空间的能耗标度关系。

Method: 利用覆盖/管状邻域估计给出路径长度下界；通过 Cauchy–Schwarz 不等式将路径长度与二次慢驱动作用量关联，导出过剩功标度律；推导两类微观摩擦张量（三级马尔可夫跳变过程、过阻尼谐振阱）；分析栅格扫描和 Morton/Z-order 路径的代价行为，并引入奇异响应代理度量。

Result: 路径长度下界 $L_g[H_\varepsilon] \ge C_g \varepsilon^{1-d} - O(\varepsilon)$；过剩功 $W_{\rm ex}^{(2)} \ge L_\zeta^2/\tau = \Omega(\varepsilon^{2(1-d)}/\tau)$；固定预算下慢驱动时间 $\tau = \Omega(\varepsilon^{2(1-d)})$；栅格扫描 $L_\zeta \sim \Delta_g^{-1}$，$W_{\rm ex}^{(2)} \sim \Delta_g^{-2}$；实验室/模拟截断后 $L_{\rm op} = \Theta(\max\{\varepsilon,\Delta_g\}^{1-d})$。

Conclusion: 经典几何估计可转化为热力学覆盖的资源律，微扰摩擦张量可从微观动力学导出；奇异响应代理度量可诊断临界指数但并非物理摩擦张量；Morton/Z-order 保持指数但增加局域依赖性振幅。

Abstract: Continuous space-filling maps can be surjective onto higher-dimensional regions, but thermodynamic protocols are rectifiable finite-resolution paths. We study exhaustive traversal of a compact $d$-dimensional thermodynamic state-space window $(\mathcal{M},g)$ by curves $H_\varepsilon$ whose images are $\varepsilon$-dense in intrinsic distance. A standard covering/tube estimate gives $L_g[H_\varepsilon]\ge C_g\varepsilon^{1-d}-O(\varepsilon)$ for every regular $d>1$ window. The geometry is classical; the contribution is to turn it into an operational resource law for thermodynamic coverage. When the physical friction tensor $ζ$ coincides with, or uniformly dominates, the coverage metric $g$, Cauchy--Schwarz for the quadratic slow-driving action gives $W_{\rm ex}^{(2)}\ge L_ζ^2/τ=Ω(\varepsilon^{2(1-d)}/τ)$. Equivalently, at fixed quadratic excess-work budget, maintaining slow driving requires $τ=Ω(\varepsilon^{2(1-d)})$. We derive microscopic friction metrics for a detailed-balance three-state Markov jump process, $ζ_{ij}=(β/γ)(π_iδ_{ij}-π_iπ_j)$, and for an overdamped harmonic trap, $\mathrm d\ell_ζ^2=μ^{-1}\mathrm da^2+(4βμk^3)^{-1}\mathrm dk^2$. In the trap, a raster scan gives $L_ζ\simΔ_g^{-1}$ and fixed-time $W_{\rm ex}^{(2)}\simΔ_g^{-2}$, while fixed dwell time shifts the cost to acquisition time. A laboratory or simulation floor cuts off the continuum divergence as $L_{\rm op}=Θ(\max\{\varepsilon,Δ_g\}^{1-d})$. Controlled singular response-proxy metrics diagnose critical prefactors and directional integrability, but are not physical friction tensors unless derived from microscopic dynamics. Morton/Z-order preserves the exponent while increasing locality-dependent amplitudes.

</details>


### [122] [Poisson-shot-noise hybrid machines: efficiency and quasistatic divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29838)
*Rita Majumdar,Costantino Di Bello,Ralf Metzler,Rahul Marathe,Édgar Roldán*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由过阻尼布朗粒子在谐振势阱中与热（被动）和活性（非热）浴同时接触构成的微观活性热机，其中活性浴由泊松散粒噪声（PSN）建模。通过解析计算和数值模拟，分析了准静态循环协议（两冲程和斯特林类引擎）的热力学性能。发现特定参数下系统表现为"混合机器"，同时作为热机和制冷机运行。在整体表现为引擎的参数区域，平均总提取功除以冷浴总吸热可能超越基于被动浴温度的卡诺效率。采用活性热机第二定律，引入信息论度量——准静态散度——来定义真正的效率（受卡诺效率约束）。分析了PSN散粒率和位置分布非高斯性对效率的影响，发现最优散粒率以及效率与峰度之间非双射的有趣关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活性浴中微观热机的热力学性能极限，特别是当活性浴由泊松散粒噪声建模时，系统表现出的混合机器行为以及可能超越传统卡诺效率的现象，并建立正确的效率度量框架。

Method: 采用过阻尼朗之万方程描述粒子动力学，活性浴力用泊松散粒噪声（PSN）建模，通过解析计算和数值模拟相结合的方法，研究准静态两冲程和斯特林类循环协议下的热力学性能。引入准静态散度作为信息论度量，分析PSN散粒率、非高斯性（峰度）与效率之间的关系。

Result: 发现系统可在特定参数范围内同时作为热机和制冷机的混合机器运行；整体引擎模式下平均效率可超越传统卡诺效率；存在最优PSN散粒率使效率最大化；效率与峰度呈现非双射关系。

Conclusion: 活性热机的正确效率应基于信息论度量（准静态散度）定义，受卡诺效率约束；PSN散粒率和非高斯性是调控引擎性能的关键参数；揭示了活性热机中非平衡统计特性与热力学性能之间的复杂关联。

Abstract: We study stochastic models of a microscopic active heat engine, comprised of an overdamped Brownian particle trapped in a harmonic potential, and in simultaneous contact with thermal (passive) and athermal (active) baths. The interaction with the active bath is modeled as a stochastic force described by Poisson shot-noise (PSN) having a specified amplitude distribution. With analytical calculations and numerical simulations, we study the thermodynamic performance of the machine to quasistatic cyclic protocols analogous to those running two-stroke and Stirling-like engines. For specific parameter ranges, the thermodynamic behavior is that of a $\textit{hybrid machine}$, simultaneously operating as a heat engine with respect to the passive/active baths and as a refrigerator with respect to the passive/active baths. Focusing on the parameter region where the overall performance is such of an engine, we show that the average total extracted work per cycle divided by average total heat intake from the cold baths per cycle may surpass the Carnot efficiency associated with the temperature of the passive baths. Applying the second law for active heat engines, we focus on a bona fide efficiency (bounded by Carnot's efficiency) that incorporates an information-theoretic metric $\mathcal{I}-$ which we call $\textit{quasistatic divergence}-$ quantifying how distinguishable are the engine's statistics in the quasistatic limit with respect to a continually changing equilibrium distribution. We analyze, with theory and numerical simulations, how the PSN shot rate and the degree of non-Gaussianity in the particle position distribution influence the efficiency of the engine, and explore the correlation between non-Gaussianity and efficiency. Our findings reveal optimal PSN shot rates maximizing the engine's efficiency and an intriguing non-bijective relation between efficiency and kurtosis

</details>


### [123] [Surviving the Attack of the Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30235)
*Denis S. Grebenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究自催化复制粒子（分裂产生克隆体）在部分反应性目标上的最快首次反应时间（FRT）统计特性，建立了非线性积分方程求解生存概率，推导了平均FRT的上下界，并通过数值模拟揭示了自催化搜索的优势与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩散粒子在遇到催化表面时可分裂为独立克隆体的种群动力学模型，探究这种自催化复制机制如何影响多个并行搜索粒子中最快首次反应时间的统计规律，以理解病毒攻击等过程中的加速搜索效应。

Method: 建立了生存概率的非线性积分方程；分析了FRT的概率密度及其矩；推导了平均FRT关于系统参数（目标反应性、催化速率、扩散系数等）的上下界；在基本几何设置下进行数值求解。

Result: 自催化复制能快速增加搜索者数量，从而显著加速扩散搜索过程；数值模拟揭示了该自催化搜索机制的优势和局限性。

Conclusion: 自催化复制可大幅加速扩散搜索，但存在一定局限性，需要根据系统参数平衡复制速率与搜索效率。

Abstract: We consider a population dynamics model in which each diffusing particle that hits a catalytic surface can split into two independent copies (clones). The particles of such a growing-in-size population search in parallel for a hidden partially reactive target to trigger a reaction event (e.g., a viral attack). We investigate the statistics of the fastest first-reaction time (FRT) among all the particles. We establish a nonlinear integral equation for the survival probability and then analyze the associated probability density of the FRT and its moments. Lower and upper bounds on the mean FRT are then deduced in terms of the system parameters (target reactivity, catalytic rate, diffusivity, etc.). Because autocatalytic replication can rapidly increase the number of searchers, it can substantially accelerate the diffusive search. We solve the nonlinear equations numerically in a basic geometric setting and reveal advantages and limitations on the autocatalytic search.

</details>


### [124] [Robust secret storage in networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30261)
*Vinko Zlatić*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式秘密存储的正式框架，通过最小信息承载子图（MICS）优化鲁棒性函数，平衡网络退化下的生存性与抗攻击性，并构建了半局部优化方法，在极限情况下映射为有效自旋哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有秘密存储方案缺乏统一的形式化框架来同时处理网络退化（节点/边失效）和敌手攻击（信息泄露）两个竞争性需求，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 引入鲁棒性函数权衡生存性与抗攻击性；推导基于最小信息承载子图（MICS）的生存性精确表示；利用MICS设计半局部优化动力学，避免全局网络信息依赖；揭示极限情况下与自旋哈密顿量的自然映射关系。

Result: 建立了生存性的MICS精确表示，据此设计了无需全局知识的半局部优化方法，并发现了鲁棒性函数与自旋哈密顿量在极限情况下的等价关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为分布式秘密存储提供了统一的理论基础，半局部方法适用于大规模网络，自旋映射则开辟了统计物理工具分析安全存储问题的途径。

Abstract: The problem of storing secure information on a network is studied. A formal framework for distributed secret storage is introduced, and possible applications in technological and social systems are discussed. The problem is formulated as the optimization of a robustness functional in which two competing requirements are balanced: survivability under network-degrading processes and resistance to adversarial compromise. An exact representation of survivability is derived in terms of minimal information-carrying subgraphs (MICS), which provide a reduced description of the reconstruction events relevant to the stored information. This representation is then used to construct semi-local optimization methods whose dynamics do not require global knowledge of the network structure. Finally, it is shown that, in a limiting case, the robustness functional can be mapped naturally to an effective spin Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [125] [A Field-Theoretic Framework for Work Statistics and Universal Scaling in Non-equilibrium Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30503)
*Yanbo Qiao,Ruohan Xu,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过场论框架和动态重整化群方法，研究了O(N)模型在临界驱动下的功统计量，推导了Kibble-Zurek标度律和工作累积量的普适标度指数，并验证了精确高斯解和数值模拟，从而将随机热力学与重整化群理论联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要依赖冻结启发式论证来理解临界驱动系统中的Kibble-Zurek标度律，缺乏从第一性原理出发的严格推导。本文旨在建立场论框架，从重整化群流的角度统一描述驱动通过临界点的O(N)模型中的非平衡功统计量。

Method: 采用场论框架和动态重整化群方法，分析复合幂算子的重整化群流；推导功累积量的标度行为，区分孤立量子系统与开放量子/经典系统的不同标度指数；利用精确高斯解和数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 建立了第n阶功累积量密度的普适标度律c_n ~ τ_Q^{-α_n}，其中孤立量子系统α_n = p(d+nz)ν/(1+pzν)，开放量子/经典系统α_n = pdν/(1+pzν)。该结果从第一性原理推导得到，并通过高斯解和数值模拟验证。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了非平衡功统计量的场论框架，弥合了随机热力学与重整化群理论之间的鸿沟，为理解临界驱动系统中远离平衡的功统计提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a field-theoretic framework for work statistics in $O(N)$ models driven through criticality. By analyzing the dynamic renormalization group flow of composite power operators, we find the Kibble-Zurek scaling laws as a natural consequence of the flow, and we derive the scaling of work cumulants relevant to Kibble-Zurek scaling of topological defects from first principles, bypassing heuristic freeze-out argument. This yields the universal scaling $c_n \sim τ_Q^{-α_n}$ for the $n$-th work cumulant density: isolated quantum systems exhibit a scaling of $α_n = p(d+nz)ν/(1+pzν)$, whereas open quantum and classical systems undergo a dimensional collapse to $α_n = pdν/(1+pzν)$. Validated by exact Gaussian solutions and numerical simulations, our theory establishes a foundation for general work statistics far from equilibrium, thereby bridging stochastic thermodynamics and the renormalization group theory.

</details>


### [126] [Why can genetic algorithms work in high-dimensional search spaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30619)
*Stephen Whitelam*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明精英$(1+M)$遗传算法在小突变极限下等效于带各向异性高斯白噪声的截断梯度下降法。遗传算法无需显式计算梯度即可沿损失梯度方向演化，但速度慢于梯度下降，其减速受损失函数Hessian矩阵有效秩控制而非参数数量。对于神经网络损失函数中常见的集中Hessian谱，有效秩远小于参数数量，这解释了遗传算法可扩展至大搜索空间的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解遗传算法为何能在高维大规模搜索空间中有效工作，特别是神经网络优化场景，揭示其与梯度下降法的内在联系。

Method: 理论分析：在小突变极限下推导精英$(1+M)$遗传算法的有效动力学，将其映射为带各向异性高斯白噪声的截断梯度下降，并分析Hessian矩阵有效秩对算法速度的影响。

Result: 遗传算法在小突变极限下等价于截断梯度下降加各向异性噪声；算法减速由Hessian有效秩而非参数数量控制；对于神经网络常见的集中Hessian谱，有效秩远小于参数数，遗传算法可扩展至大搜索空间。

Conclusion: 遗传算法本质上是一种隐式梯度下降方法，其扩展性源于损失景观的局部几何特性（Hessian有效秩低）而非搜索空间维度本身。

Abstract: We show that the effective dynamics of the elitist $(1+M)$ genetic algorithm is, in the limit of small mutations, clipped gradient descent on the loss in the presence of anisotropic Gaussian white noise. In expectation, therefore, a simple mutation-selection genetic algorithm follows the gradient of the loss, without explicit calculation of gradients and without averaging over loss evaluations. The genetic algorithm is slower than gradient descent because of the noise that acts in directions transverse to the gradient. However, this slowdown is controlled not by the number of parameters of the search space but by the effective rank of the Hessian of the loss function. For the concentrated Hessian spectra observed in neural-network loss functions the effective rank can be far smaller than the number of parameters, which may explain why genetic algorithms can scale to large search spaces.

</details>
